===== FILE: modules/cedar_engine_0000.py =====
"""Synthetic coverage aggregation for the fictional cedar_engine_0000 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_cedar_engine_0000(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 28) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_cedar_engine_0000(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: crates/brisk_ledger_0001/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_brisk_ledger_0001(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 371 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_brisk_ledger_0001(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

===== FILE: web/ochre_garden_0002.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeOchreGarden0002(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 800) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

===== FILE: notes/kindled_upland_0006.md =====
# Design record: kindled upland 0006

## Context

In a deliberately fictional exercise, a community kitchen lab compares a ledger of bounded experiments while keeping the plan bounded to one working session, then marks the effort column for a separate reading.
Because recovery can change the interpretation, a volunteer map room compares a table of simulated readings while preserving the original ordering of evidence.

## Decision

Because latency can change the interpretation, the meadow survey group revises the latest material samples while keeping uncertain values visibly marked.
For observation 75, the ceramics cooperative documents a set of clearly reversible changes; the note labels yield as simulated rather than measured.

## Alternatives retained

1. For observation 121, a public garden committee reorders the remaining design alternatives; the note labels variance as simulated rather than measured.
2. Because drift can change the interpretation, a community kitchen lab reorders the queue of ordinary repairs so later readers can reconstruct the reasoning.
3. Because drift can change the interpretation, a community kitchen lab documents a table of simulated readings without hiding incomplete or inconvenient outcomes.
4. For observation 124, the hilltop instrument bench sketches an intentionally small operating plan; the note labels coverage as simulated rather than measured.
5. The working record says that the paper engineering guild reorders the workshop's fictional dataset. It does so while preserving the original ordering of evidence.
6. The working record says that a library preservation unit documents a ledger of bounded experiments. It does so while preserving the original ordering of evidence.

## Review matrix

| Question | Answer | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| effort | revise | synthetic row 0006-00 |
| coverage | retain | synthetic row 0006-01 |
| variance | retain | synthetic row 0006-02 |
| drift | retain | synthetic row 0006-03 |
| variance | revise | synthetic row 0006-04 |
| recovery | retain | synthetic row 0006-05 |

This record is fictional, original, and dedicated under CC0-1.0.

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_paper_notebook_0010(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 451 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_paper_notebook_0010(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);

export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;

  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeNorthDelta0011(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 896) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_cedar_orchard_0019(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 242 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {

            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },

===== FILE: web/tidal_beacon_0020.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeTidalBeacon0020(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 352) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

===== FILE: modules/ochre_registry_0027.py =====
"""Synthetic variance aggregation for the fictional ochre_registry_0027 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_ochre_registry_0027(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 61) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_ochre_registry_0027(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: crates/amber_notebook_0028/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_amber_notebook_0028(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 168 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_amber_notebook_0028(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

===== FILE: web/meadow_vessel_0029.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeMeadowVessel0029(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 320) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

===== FILE: crates/linen_notebook_0046/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_linen_notebook_0046(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 173 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_linen_notebook_0046(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

===== FILE: web/paper_workshop_0047.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizePaperWorkshop0047(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 256) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

===== FILE: modules/brisk_trellis_0054.py =====
"""Synthetic drift aggregation for the fictional brisk_trellis_0054 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_brisk_trellis_0054(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 191) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_brisk_trellis_0054(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_paper_workshop_0055(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 419 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

===== FILE: modules/silver_trellis_0072.py =====
"""Synthetic yield aggregation for the fictional silver_trellis_0072 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_silver_trellis_0072(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 26) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_silver_trellis_0072(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: crates/evening_trellis_0073/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_evening_trellis_0073(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 184 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_evening_trellis_0073(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

===== FILE: web/indigo_signal_0074.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeIndigoSignal0074(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 256) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_paper_matrix_0091(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }

===== FILE: web/harbor_index_0092.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeHarborIndex0092(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 480) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

===== FILE: modules/willow_garden_0099.py =====
"""Synthetic variance aggregation for the fictional willow_garden_0099 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_willow_garden_0099(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 194) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_willow_garden_0099(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: crates/north_folio_0100/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_north_folio_0100(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 449 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_north_folio_0100(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

===== FILE: modules/quiet_notebook_0117.py =====
"""Synthetic variance aggregation for the fictional quiet_notebook_0117 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_quiet_notebook_0117(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 39) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_quiet_notebook_0117(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: crates/meadow_matrix_0118/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_meadow_matrix_0118(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 459 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_meadow_matrix_0118(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

===== FILE: web/brisk_signal_0119.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeBriskSignal0119(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 672) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

# Design record: drift ledger 0123

## Context

The working record says that the neighborhood weather desk classifies a set of reversible decisions. It does so without treating an estimate as an observation.
Because stability can change the interpretation, a community kitchen lab estimates the queue of ordinary repairs without hiding incomplete or inconvenient outcomes.

## Decision

Because variance can change the interpretation, a fictional transit studio balances the latest material samples with an explicit stopping condition for the experiment.
Because drift can change the interpretation, the meadow survey group examines the order of independent work items using only the fictional measurements in this document.

## Alternatives retained

1. In a deliberately fictional exercise, the meadow survey group catalogs the differences between two mock trials using only the fictional measurements in this document, then marks the yield column for a separate reading.
2. The neighborhood weather desk maps a sequence of labeled measurements after comparing the calm and windy trial periods.
3. A local history workshop groups several competing explanations before any irreversible step is proposed.
4. A library preservation unit balances the queue of ordinary repairs with separate notes for fact, inference, and preference.
5. For observation 2465, the harbor planning circle rechecks a set of clearly reversible changes; the note labels stability as simulated rather than measured.
6. Because margin can change the interpretation, a local history workshop groups the workshop's fictional dataset with separate notes for fact, inference, and preference.

===== FILE: web/meadow_notebook_0137.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeMeadowNotebook0137(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 640) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

# Design record: juniper delta 0141

## Context

The working record says that a small observatory crew estimates a sequence of labeled measurements. It does so after every source row has been checked.
The working record says that a local history workshop balances the acceptance rules for the next review. It does so with separate notes for fact, inference, and preference.

## Decision

In a deliberately fictional exercise, a public garden committee estimates the remaining design alternatives after comparing the calm and windy trial periods, then marks the margin column for a separate reading.
The working record says that the harbor planning circle documents a sequence of labeled measurements. It does so while keeping uncertain values visibly marked.

## Alternatives retained

1. The working record says that a public garden committee balances a sequence of labeled measurements. It does so using only the fictional measurements in this document.
2. The lantern design office summarizes the queue of ordinary repairs while keeping uncertain values visibly marked.
3. Because noise can change the interpretation, the cedar workshop compares the order of independent work items while keeping uncertain values visibly marked.
4. The working record says that a public garden committee revises the latest material samples. It does so while keeping the plan bounded to one working session.
5. The working record says that the cedar workshop summarizes a hand-drawn seasonal model. It does so before any irreversible step is proposed.
6. Because effort can change the interpretation, the morning logistics crew revises an inventory of modest risks before the afternoon review begins.

===== FILE: modules/silver_catalog_0144.py =====
"""Synthetic effort aggregation for the fictional silver_catalog_0144 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_silver_catalog_0144(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 178) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_silver_catalog_0144(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: crates/paper_quarry_0145/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_paper_quarry_0145(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 118 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_paper_quarry_0145(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

pub fn fold_quiet_ledger_0154(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 427 {

===== FILE: modules/paper_kiln_0162.py =====
"""Synthetic stability aggregation for the fictional paper_kiln_0162 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_paper_kiln_0162(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 142) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_paper_kiln_0162(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: crates/indigo_harvest_0163/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_indigo_harvest_0163(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 464 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_indigo_harvest_0163(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

===== FILE: web/indigo_orchard_0164.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeIndigoOrchard0164(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 224) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

===== FILE: web/tidal_beacon_0182.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeTidalBeacon0182(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 384) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

===== FILE: modules/linen_garden_0189.py =====
"""Synthetic latency aggregation for the fictional linen_garden_0189 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_linen_garden_0189(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 140) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_linen_garden_0189(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: crates/umber_workshop_0190/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_umber_workshop_0190(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 64 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_umber_workshop_0190(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

===== FILE: web/river_quarry_0191.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeRiverQuarry0191(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 448) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

===== FILE: modules/tidal_atlas_0207.py =====
"""Synthetic drift aggregation for the fictional tidal_atlas_0207 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_tidal_atlas_0207(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 109) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_tidal_atlas_0207(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: crates/tidal_workshop_0208/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_tidal_workshop_0208(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 225 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_tidal_workshop_0208(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

===== FILE: web/drift_registry_0209.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeDriftRegistry0209(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 640) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

===== FILE: notes/granite_beacon_0213.md =====
# Design record: granite beacon 0213

## Context

In a deliberately fictional exercise, an independent archive team examines a set of reversible decisions and leaves a concise explanation for each exception, then marks the latency column for a separate reading.
A rotating review panel examines a hand-drawn seasonal model so the report remains useful after the details change.

## Decision

The working record says that a coastal ecology class documents the acceptance rules for the next review. It does so while keeping uncertain values visibly marked.
Because effort can change the interpretation, a fictional transit studio maps a table of simulated readings after comparing the calm and windy trial periods.

## Alternatives retained

1. In a deliberately fictional exercise, the ceramics cooperative compares the differences between two mock trials and assigns a plain-language meaning to each column, then marks the drift column for a separate reading.
2. For observation 4262, a small observatory crew reviews the workshop's fictional dataset; the note labels latency as simulated rather than measured.
3. In a deliberately fictional exercise, a local history workshop maps a set of clearly reversible changes and assigns a plain-language meaning to each column, then marks the latency column for a separate reading.
4. A fictional transit studio groups the acceptance rules for the next review so a second crew can repeat the exercise independently.
5. A library preservation unit catalogs a table of simulated readings so a second crew can repeat the exercise independently.
6. In a deliberately fictional exercise, the river modelers reconciles a collection of field-note fragments without hiding incomplete or inconvenient outcomes, then marks the recovery column for a separate reading.

## Review matrix

| Question | Answer | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| drift | revise | synthetic row 0213-00 |
| drift | revise | synthetic row 0213-01 |
| effort | retain | synthetic row 0213-02 |
| recovery | retain | synthetic row 0213-03 |
| effort | retain | synthetic row 0213-04 |
| margin | retain | synthetic row 0213-05 |

This record is fictional, original, and dedicated under CC0-1.0.

pub fn fold_evening_atlas_0217(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 354 {

    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };

===== FILE: modules/linen_yardstick_0234.py =====
"""Synthetic readiness aggregation for the fictional linen_yardstick_0234 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_linen_yardstick_0234(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 120) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_linen_yardstick_0234(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: crates/drift_beacon_0235/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_drift_beacon_0235(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 499 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_drift_beacon_0235(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

===== FILE: web/north_parcel_0236.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeNorthParcel0236(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 128) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

pub fn fold_kindled_notebook_0244(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 94 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {

            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;

===== FILE: modules/indigo_atlas_0252.py =====
"""Synthetic stability aggregation for the fictional indigo_atlas_0252 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_indigo_atlas_0252(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 190) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_indigo_atlas_0252(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: crates/indigo_engine_0253/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_indigo_engine_0253(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 441 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_indigo_engine_0253(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

===== FILE: web/drift_orchard_0254.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeDriftOrchard0254(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 800) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_velvet_trellis_0262(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 201 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

===== FILE: modules/kindled_ledger_0279.py =====
"""Synthetic coverage aggregation for the fictional kindled_ledger_0279 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_kindled_ledger_0279(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 157) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_kindled_ledger_0279(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: crates/granite_yardstick_0280/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_granite_yardstick_0280(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 472 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_granite_yardstick_0280(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

===== FILE: web/granite_yardstick_0281.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeGraniteYardstick0281(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 416) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

export function summarizeAmberVessel0290(

def summarize_cedar_registry_0297(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 54) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_cedar_registry_0297(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: crates/north_beacon_0298/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_north_beacon_0298(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 281 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_north_beacon_0298(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

===== FILE: web/silver_delta_0299.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeSilverDelta0299(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 224) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_cedar_trellis_0306(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 157) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_cedar_trellis_0306(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: modules/north_harvest_0324.py =====
"""Synthetic recovery aggregation for the fictional north_harvest_0324 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_north_harvest_0324(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 41) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_north_harvest_0324(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: crates/meadow_quarry_0325/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_meadow_quarry_0325(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 297 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_meadow_quarry_0325(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

===== FILE: web/cedar_notebook_0326.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeCedarNotebook0326(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 768) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,

pub fn fold_meadow_kiln_0334(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 362 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeCedarLedger0335(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 736) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: crates/linen_index_0343/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_linen_index_0343(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 296 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_linen_index_0343(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

===== FILE: web/cedar_matrix_0344.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeCedarMatrix0344(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 576) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

===== FILE: modules/north_kiln_0351.py =====
"""Synthetic variance aggregation for the fictional north_kiln_0351 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_north_kiln_0351(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 51) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_north_kiln_0351(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: crates/paper_delta_0352/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_paper_delta_0352(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 488 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_paper_delta_0352(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);

===== FILE: modules/granite_garden_0369.py =====
"""Synthetic clarity aggregation for the fictional granite_garden_0369 module."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Reading:
    label: str
    value: int
    accepted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Summary:
    accepted: int
    rejected: int
    total: int


def summarize_granite_garden_0369(readings: list[Reading], ceiling: int = 166) -> Summary:
    """Classify bounded mock readings without discarding rejected rows."""
    if ceiling < 1:
        raise ValueError("ceiling must be positive")
    accepted = 0
    rejected = 0
    total = 0
    for reading in readings:
        if reading.value < 0:
            raise ValueError("reading values must be non-negative")
        total += reading.value
        if reading.accepted and reading.value <= ceiling:
            accepted += 1
        else:
            rejected += 1
    return Summary(accepted=accepted, rejected=rejected, total=total)


def render_granite_garden_0369(summary: Summary) -> str:
    fields = (
        f"accepted={summary.accepted}",
        f"rejected={summary.rejected}",
        f"total={summary.total}",
    )
    return ", ".join(fields)

===== FILE: crates/river_folio_0370/src/lib.rs =====
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub sequence: u32,
    pub magnitude: u64,
    pub complete: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct Totals {
    pub complete: usize,
    pub incomplete: usize,
    pub magnitude: u64,
}

pub fn fold_river_folio_0370(samples: &[Sample]) -> Result<Totals, &'static str> {
    if samples.len() > 319 {
        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_river_folio_0370(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }
}

===== FILE: web/meadow_orchard_0371.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeMeadowOrchard0371(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 608) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}

        return Err("sample collection exceeds the synthetic bound");
    }
    let mut totals = Totals { complete: 0, incomplete: 0, magnitude: 0 };
    for sample in samples {
        totals.magnitude = totals.magnitude
            .checked_add(sample.magnitude)
            .ok_or("magnitude overflow")?;
        if sample.complete {
            totals.complete += 1;
        } else {
            totals.incomplete += 1;
        }
    }
    Ok(totals)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_samples_remain_in_totals() {
        let samples = [
            Sample { sequence: 1, magnitude: 8, complete: true },
            Sample { sequence: 2, magnitude: 13, complete: false },
        ];
        let totals = fold_indigo_delta_0388(&samples).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(totals.complete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.incomplete, 1);
        assert_eq!(totals.magnitude, 21);
    }

===== FILE: web/river_trellis_0389.ts =====
export type Observation = Readonly<{
  sequence: number;
  category: "calm" | "changing" | "uncertain";
  reading: number;
}>;

export type WindowSummary = Readonly<{
  count: number;
  minimum: number | null;
  maximum: number | null;
  span: number | null;
}>;

export function summarizeRiverTrellis0389(
  rows: readonly Observation[],
): WindowSummary {
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { count: 0, minimum: null, maximum: null, span: null };
  }
  if (rows.length > 320) {
    throw new Error("synthetic observation window is too wide");
  }
  let minimum = rows[0].reading;
  let maximum = rows[0].reading;
  for (const row of rows.slice(1)) {
    minimum = Math.min(minimum, row.reading);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, row.reading);
  }
  return { count: rows.length, minimum, maximum, span: maximum - minimum };
}
